- **pacbio-ccs-read-level-5mc-classification**: Use when calling 5mCpG methylation states at single-molecule (per-read) resolution from long (≥10 Kb) PacBio CCS/HiFi reads (subreads.bam or hifi.bam), or when benchmarking read-level 5mC classifiers on M.SssI-methylated vs PCR-treated control datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-ccs-read-level-5mc-classification))
- **pacbio-ccs-read-processing**: Use when input is PacBio Iso-seq CCS read FASTA from long-read transcriptome sequencing and it must be prepared/filtered before TAGET alignment, annotation, or fusion detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-ccs-read-processing))
- **pacbio-hifi-alignment-index-generation**: Use when preparing a scywalker/genomecomb reference directory (via scywalker_makerefdir or a downloaded genomecomb refdb) for PacBio HiFi single-cell FASTQ input; without this step the reference only carries the default ONT minimap2 index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-hifi-alignment-index-generation))
- **pacbio-hifi-pass-number-sampling**: Use when simulating PacBio HiFi amplicon subreads whose number of passes (np) must match a real sequencing run's distribution, since PBSIM3 supports only one pass number per invocation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/pacbio-hifi-pass-number-sampling))
- **pacbio-iso-seq-read-quality-parsing**: Use when running IsoCon's statistical filtering (stat_filter) on targeted Iso-Seq FLNC reads in fastq/fasta and per-read error probabilities must be extracted from Phred quality values to compute candidate-significance p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-iso-seq-read-quality-parsing))
- **pacbio-isoseq-gold-standard-generation**: Use when matched PacBio HiFi and Nanopore R10 reads exist for the same single-cell 5' cDNA library and you need an accuracy gold standard for evaluating isoform quantification methods like Longcell, FLAMES, Sicelore2, or wf-single-cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pacbio-isoseq-gold-standard-generation))
- **pacbio-kinetic-read-filtering**: Use when input is a raw PacBio CCS BAM with kinetic tags (fi, ri, fp, rp) that must be quality-filtered before Z-Calling base calling, SVM read classification, or metagenome dZ-source detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-kinetic-read-filtering))
- **pacbio-kinetic-signal-extraction**: Use when PacBio CCS BAM files contain kinetic tags (fi, ri, fp, rp) and the goal is per-base PW/IPD feature extraction around A/Z target bases for dZ-DNA detection or kinetic shift analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-kinetic-signal-extraction))
- **pacbio-long-read-rnaseq-downsampling**: Use when deciding the minimum PacBio long-read RNA-seq depth needed for dsRID training/prediction: you have a full-depth BAM (e.g. 4.27M reads) and must justify how many reads (1.5–2M plateau) suffice. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pacbio-long-read-rnaseq-downsampling))
- **pacbio-long-read-simulation**: Use when you need ground-truth-controlled hybrid sequencing data (short + long reads) to benchmark genome assemblers on misassemblies, NGA50, genome fraction, runtime, and memory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-long-read-simulation))
- **pacbio-metagenome-read-correction**: Use when raw PacBio CLR (or ONT) FASTQ/FASTA reads from mixed samples such as metagenomes or polyploid genomes must be error-corrected without masking true low-frequency haplotype variants. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-metagenome-read-correction))
- **pacbio-orthogonal-methylation-label-construction**: Use when you need labeled methylated/unmethylated positions from PacBio-validated motif sites (e.g., E. coli GATC) to train or score ONT nanopore m6A classifiers on current-deviation features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-orthogonal-methylation-label-construction))
- **pacbio-read-error-correction**: Use when raw PacBio or ONT long reads (FASTA/FASTQ) contain sequencing errors that must be reduced via self-correction before assembly, mapping, or downstream benchmarking, without short-read or reference-based correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-read-error-correction))
- **pacbio-read-pileup-allelotyping**: Use when calling SNVs from error-prone PacBio SMRT (or ONT) whole-genome BAM alignments, where candidate variants must be detected from a read pileup and read-level allele observations must be extracted for haplotype-informed genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-read-pileup-allelotyping))
- **pacbio-reference-motif-curation**: Use when validating de novo methylation motifs detected from ONT data (e.g. Snapper output final_motifs_[strand]_[contig].fasta) against an established PacBio methylome reference for the same strain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-reference-motif-curation))
- **pacbio-subread-ccs-generation**: Use when input data are PacBio subread BAM files from Iso-Seq (single-molecule, long-read RNA) sequencing and the goal is to produce circular consensus (HiFi) reads as the entry point to the nf-core/isoseq FLNC preprocessing chain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pacbio-subread-ccs-generation))
- **pacchettino**: Official reference for pacchettino, the file-based job queue for the D programming language (producers and consumers across processes and threads, crash recovery, delayed jobs, retries, status tracking). Use it whenever the user asks about pacchettino, or about job queues, background jobs or work queues in D. ([trikko/pacchettino](https://github.com/trikko/pacchettino/tree/HEAD/docs) · ★3)
- **paccs-tech-advisory-council**: Supports the Advisory Council - provides strategic guidance, expert review of initiatives, risk identification, and cross-committee coordination. Use when the user needs strategic planning, initiative review, risk assessment, or organizational guidance. ([dougdevitre/paccs](https://github.com/dougdevitre/paccs/tree/HEAD/skills/advisory-council))
- **paccs-tech-advocate**: Primary support skill for parents and families experiencing parental alienation. Provides documentation help, safety planning, court preparation, resource navigation, empowerment messaging, and crisis support. Use when the user asks for advocacy, resources, documentation, support planning, or help navigating alienation. ([dougdevitre/paccs](https://github.com/dougdevitre/paccs/tree/HEAD/skills/advocate))
- **paccs-tech-crisis-safety**: Crisis response and safety planning for parents and children affected by parental alienation, contact denial, false allegations, and coercive control. Use when the user is in crisis, facing emergency custody issues, or needs immediate safety guidance. ([dougdevitre/paccs](https://github.com/dougdevitre/paccs/tree/HEAD/skills/crisis-safety))
- **paccs-tech-curriculum-training**: Supports the Curriculum Development & Training Committee - designs training programs, CEU/CLE modules, learning objectives, and educational materials for professionals. Use when the user needs training curriculum, course design, learning assessments, or professional education content. ([dougdevitre/paccs](https://github.com/dougdevitre/paccs/tree/HEAD/skills/curriculum-training))
- **paccs-tech-documentation**: Specialized documentation assistant for parents building court-ready evidence of parental alienation, false allegations, coercive control, and contact interference. Use when the user needs help documenting incidents, building timelines, organizing evidence, or creating court-ready materials. ([dougdevitre/paccs](https://github.com/dougdevitre/paccs/tree/HEAD/skills/documentation))
- **paccs-tech-legal-advisory**: Supports the Legal Advisory Committee - provides guidance on legal standards, statutes, case law, and policy issues affecting child-centered safety and family court practices. Use when the user needs legal research, legislation review, policy briefs, or courtroom resource templates. ([dougdevitre/paccs](https://github.com/dougdevitre/paccs/tree/HEAD/skills/legal-advisory))
- **paccs-tech-lived-experience**: Supports the Lived Experience Committee - helps families share testimony safely, reviews materials for survivor accuracy, identifies system gaps, and creates trauma-informed educational content. Use when the user needs help with testimony, case summaries, system accountability, or survivor-informed review. ([dougdevitre/paccs](https://github.com/dougdevitre/paccs/tree/HEAD/skills/lived-experience))
- **paccs-tech-policy-education**: Supports the Policy, Education & Public Outreach Committee - creates educational materials, legislative briefings, public campaigns, and evidence-based resources for courts, legislators, and the public. Use when the user needs policy education content, outreach materials, or legislative communication. ([dougdevitre/paccs](https://github.com/dougdevitre/paccs/tree/HEAD/skills/policy-education))
- **paccs-tech-research-integrity**: Supports the Research Integrity Committee - verifies facts and citations, monitors misinformation, tracks legislation, and produces evidence-based summaries and myth-busting content. Use when the user needs fact-checking, research summaries, legislation tracking, or misinformation analysis. ([dougdevitre/paccs](https://github.com/dougdevitre/paccs/tree/HEAD/skills/research-integrity))
- **paccs-tech-reunification**: Supports families going through parent-child reunification after alienation or separation. Provides reconnection planning, age-appropriate communication guidance, therapist coordination templates, and progress tracking. Use when the user needs help with reunification, reconnecting with a child, or rebuilding a parent-child relationship. ([dougdevitre/paccs](https://github.com/dougdevitre/paccs/tree/HEAD/skills/reunification))
- **pace**: Change how visibly Claude surfaces analytical work during L3+ analyses. Use this skill whenever the user invokes `/pace`, `/pace guided`, `/pace narrated`, `/pace autopilot`, or says things like "slow down and walk me through", "pause between steps", "just run it silently", "narrate each step", "don't stop to ask me", "stop narrating". Pace is orthogonal to complexity level — it changes surfacing, not which agents run.
